\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}}{s}\cdot\frac{1}{1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{kj}}}\cdot\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)\cdot\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{k\ell}}{s}\Big)\cdot\prod_{{j\neq(i,\ell)\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)~~=:{\rm TermII(b)_{\ell}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad={\rm TermII(a)_{\ell}}+{\rm TermII(b)_{\ell}}

(H.29)

Thus,

\displaystyle{\rm TermII}=\sum_{\ell\neq i\in[k-1]}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermII(a)_{\ell}}\right]+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermII(b)_{\ell}}\right]=:\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermII(a)}\right]+N_{i},

(H.30)

where we defined

\displaystyle N_{i}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}}{s}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)}{1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{kj}}}\cdot\sum_{\ell\neq i\in[k-1]}\left\{\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{k\ell}}{s}\Big)\cdot\prod_{{j\neq(i,\ell)\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)\right\}\right]<0.

(H.31)

and we remark for later use that

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermII(a)}\right]=\sum_{\ell\neq i\in[k-1]}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermII(a)_{\ell}}\right]<0.

(H.32)

Second, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)}{\mathrm{d}H_{ki}}=-\frac{e^{H_{ki}}}{\left(1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{kj}}\right)^{2}}\cdot\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)\cdot\prod_{{j\neq i\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\frac{\mathrm{d}\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)}{\mathrm{d}H_{ki}}\cdot\frac{1}{1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{kj}}}\cdot\prod_{{j\neq i\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad~=-\frac{e^{H_{ki}}}{\left(1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{kj}}\right)^{2}}\cdot\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)\cdot\prod_{{j\neq i\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)~~=:{\rm TermI(a)}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}}{s}\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\right)\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)\cdot\frac{1}{1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{kj}}}\cdot\prod_{{j\neq i\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)~~=:{\rm TermI(b)},

(H.33)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad~={\rm TermI(a)}+{\rm TermI(b)},

where in the penultimate line we used the fact that
\phi^{\prime}(x)=-x\phi(x)
. Consider the two terms in (
H.33
). Clearly,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermI(a)}]<0.

(H.34)

For the second term we observe that:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermI(b)}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}}{s}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\right)\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)\right]

(H.35)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha}{s^{2}}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[H_{ki}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)\right]-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}}{s}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\bm{g}}_{k}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha}{s^{2}}\cdot A_{i}-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}}{s}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\bm{g}}_{k}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)\right].

(H.36)

Moreover, using again GIBP,
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{g}}_{k}^{2}]=1
,
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{g}}_{k}H_{kj}]=0,~j\in[k]
and the fact that
\phi^{\prime}(x)=-x\phi(x)
,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\bm{g}}_{k}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)\right]=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathrm{d}\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k]}\right)}{\mathrm{d}{\bm{g}}_{k}}\right]

\displaystyle=-\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\right)\cdot\mathcal{A}_{i}\left({\bm{g}}_{k},\{H_{kj}\}_{j\in[k-1]}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell\neq i\in[k-1]}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{k\ell}}{s}\Big)\cdot\frac{1}{1+\sum_{j\in[k-1]}e^{H_{k\ell}}}\cdot\phi\Big({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{ki}}{s}\Big)\cdot\prod_{{j\neq(i,\ell)\in[k-1]}}Q\left({\bm{g}}_{k}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha}\,H_{kj}}{s}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{s}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermI(b)}\right]+\frac{s}{\sqrt{\alpha}}N_{i},

(H.37)

where, we have recalled (
H.35
) and (
H.31
). Using (
H.37
) in (
H.36
), we find that

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermI(b)}\right]=-\frac{\alpha}{s^{2}}\cdot A_{i}-\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermI(b)}\right]-N_{i}\implies\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm TermI(b)}\right]=-\frac{\alpha}{2s^{2}}\cdot A_{i}-\frac{N_{i}}{2}.

(H.38)

We are now ready to put things together:

\displaystyle A_{i}

\displaystyle=2\cdot{\rm TermI}+{\rm TermII}\qquad\text{ by \eqref{eq:dercomp}}

\displaystyle=2\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermI(a)}]+2\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermI(b)}]+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermII(a)}]+N_{i}\qquad\text{by \eqref{eq:TermII}}

\displaystyle=2\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermI(a)}]-\frac{\alpha}{s^{2}}\cdot A_{i}-N_{i}+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermII(a)}]+N_{i}\qquad\text{by \eqref{eq:TermIb}}

\displaystyle\implies A_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{s^{2}}{s^{2}+\alpha}\left(2\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermI(a)}]+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\rm TermII(a)}]\right)

\displaystyle<0\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\text{by \eqref{eq:TermIa} and \eqref{eq:TermIIa}}.

From this, (
H.26
) and (
H.27
), we have shown that
{\cal{G}}
is strictly decreasing in
(0,\infty)
. Recalling the definition of
{\cal{G}}
in (
H.25
), this implies that
F(p,q)
is strictly increasing in
p>0
, as desired to complete the proof.

Appendix I
Weighted LS for GMM (Proof of Theorem
B.1
)

I.1
Computing
\Sigma_{w,\mu}

The WLS estimator solves:

\displaystyle\underset{\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times d},\text{ }\bm{b}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}{\min}\quad\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\left(\bm{W}\bm{X}+\bm{b}\bm{1}_{n}^{T}-\bm{Y}\right)\bm{D}\right\|_{F}^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell},\bm{b}_{\ell}}\text{ }\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\bm{D}\left(\bm{X}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell},\bm{b}_{\ell}}\text{ }\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\bm{D}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\,.

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{PO}\left(\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}\right):=\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\bm{D}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\,.

(I.1)

Identifying the AO.
By duality we have

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\mathcal{L}_{PO}\left(\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}\right)=

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\bm{s}}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{Z}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{s}^{T}\bm{u}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)

Note that the above is jointly convex in
(\bm{u},\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell})
and concave in
\bm{s}
. Thus, we consider the Auxiliary Optimization (AO) problem

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\bm{s}}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\sigma\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{s}+\sigma\left\|\bm{D}\bm{s}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{h}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{s}^{T}\bm{u}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right),

where
\bm{g}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and
\bm{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are independent Gaussian random vectors with i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
entries. Moreover, we carry out a change of variable
\bm{s}\rightarrow\bm{D}\bm{s}
to arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\bm{s}}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{s}^{T}\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\sigma\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}^{T}\bm{s}+\sigma\left\|\bm{s}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{h}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{s}^{T}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{s}^{T}\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)\,.

Simplification of the AO.

Maximizing over the direction of
\bm{s}
and setting its norm
\beta=\left\|\bm{s}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}
above we arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\max_{\bm{s}:\left\|\bm{s}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}=1}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\beta\bm{s}^{T}\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\sigma\beta\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}^{T}\bm{s}+\sigma\beta\bm{h}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\beta\bm{s}^{T}\bm{1}_{n}-\beta\bm{s}^{T}\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\beta\bm{s}^{T}\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\min_{\bm{u},\bm{w}_{\ell},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\beta\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\sigma\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\sigma\beta\bm{h}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\min_{\bm{u},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell}}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\beta\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{M}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\sigma\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\sigma\beta\bm{h}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)\,.

To continue, consider the singular value decomposition

\displaystyle\bm{M}=\bm{U}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{u}}_{1}&{\bm{u}}_{2}&\ldots&{\bm{u}}_{r}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},\ldots,\sigma_{r})\begin{bmatrix}\bm{v}^{T}_{1}\\
\bm{v}_{2}^{T}\\
\ldots\\
\bm{v}_{r}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\,,

(I.2)

with
r:=rank(\bm{M})\leq k
and define the variable
\bm{\alpha}=\bm{U}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}
and
\bm{\alpha}_{\perp}=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}
where
\bm{U}_{\perp}
is the orthogonal complement of the columns of
\bm{U}
. With these definitions the above optimization problem reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}_{\perp}}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\beta\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\left\|\bm{\alpha}_{\perp}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\sigma\beta\bm{h}^{T}\bm{U}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\beta\bm{h}^{T}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\alpha}_{\perp}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)\,.

Decomposing the optimization over
\bm{\alpha}_{\perp}
in terms of its direction and norm
\alpha_{0}=\left\|\bm{\alpha}_{\perp}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}
we arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\beta\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\sigma\beta\bm{h}^{T}\bm{U}\bm{\alpha}-\sigma\alpha_{0}\beta\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right)\,.

Since
\bm{U}^{T}\bm{h}
is
r\leq k
dimensional in our asymptotic regime the term
\frac{\bm{h}^{T}\bm{U}\bm{\alpha}}{n}
can be ignored. Also replacing
\beta
with
\beta/\sqrt{n}
we thus arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{u},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{n}}\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sigma\alpha_{0}\beta\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2n}

\displaystyle=\min_{\bm{u},b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\frac{\beta}{2n\tau}\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sigma\alpha_{0}\beta\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2n}

\displaystyle=\min_{b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{u}}\text{ }\frac{\beta}{2n\tau}\left\|\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sigma\alpha_{0}\beta\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\frac{\left\|\bm{u}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2n}\,.

Setting the derivative with respect to
\bm{u}
to zero we arrive at

\displaystyle\bm{u}=\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-1}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}\right)\,.

Plugging the latter into the above the AO simplifies to

\displaystyle\min_{b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\text{ }\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\text{trace}\left(\bm{t}^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\bm{t}\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sigma\alpha_{0}\beta\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}

where

\displaystyle\bm{t}:=\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}=\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+\sigma\sqrt{\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}}\bm{g}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}

To continue note that in our asymptotic regime we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{T}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\text{ }\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\text{ }\sqrt{\gamma}

and the cross terms can be ignored so that in an asymptotic sense

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\text{trace}\left(\bm{t}^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\bm{t}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\frac{1}{n}\sigma^{2}\left(\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\text{trace}\left(\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)

Therefore we arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\text{ }\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\sigma^{2}\left(\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}\right)\text{trace}\left(\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\right)-\sigma\alpha_{0}\beta\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)\,,

which can be rewritten in the form

\displaystyle\min_{b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\text{ }\text{ }\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\sigma^{2}\left\|\bm{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\text{trace}\left(\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\right)+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\sigma^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}\text{trace}\left(\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\right)-\alpha_{0}\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}\,.

To continue further we shall assume
\bm{D}=\text{diag}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\bm{\omega}\right)
. Note that in this case

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\text{trace}\left(\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\right)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(\bm{y}_{i}^{T}\bm{\omega})^{2}}{(\bm{y}_{i}^{T}\bm{\omega})^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{n_{\ell}}{n}\frac{\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}\text{ }\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\text{ }\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}\,.

Also,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\left(\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}\bm{Y}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{n}b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}^{T}\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\bm{Y}^{T}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+\frac{b_{\ell}^{2}}{n}\text{trace}\left(\left(\bm{I}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{D}^{-2}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\text{ }\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}\text{diag}\left(\frac{\pi_{1}\omega_{1}^{2}}{\omega_{1}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}},\frac{\pi_{2}\omega_{2}^{2}}{\omega_{2}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}},\ldots,\frac{\pi_{k}\omega_{k}^{2}}{\omega_{k}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}\right)\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2b_{\ell}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\pi_{1}\omega_{1}^{2}}{\omega_{1}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}&\frac{\pi_{2}\omega_{2}^{2}}{\omega_{2}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}&\ldots&\frac{\pi_{k}\omega_{k}^{2}}{\omega_{k}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}\end{bmatrix}\left(\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\alpha}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+b_{\ell}^{2}\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{\tau}}\right)\,.

Next define

\displaystyle\bm{A}(\eta):=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}:=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\,,

(I.3)

where

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}(\eta)=\frac{1}{\gamma}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\omega_{1}^{2}}{\omega_{1}^{2}+\eta}\\
\frac{\omega_{2}^{2}}{\omega_{2}^{2}+\eta}\\
\ldots\\
\frac{\omega_{k}^{2}}{\omega_{k}^{2}+\eta}\end{bmatrix}\,.

(I.4)

We thus arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\min_{b_{\ell}}\text{ }\max_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\text{ }\frac{\gamma\beta}{2\tau}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\gamma\beta}{2\tau}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\right)\alpha_{0}^{2}-\alpha_{0}\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}\,.

Deterministic Analysis of the AO.

Setting the derivative of the above with respect to
\alpha_{0}
to zero we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma\beta}{\tau}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\right)\alpha_{0}-\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad\alpha_{0}=\frac{\tau}{\sigma\sqrt{\gamma}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\right)}\,.

Note that the above objective has the form

\displaystyle f\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)-\alpha_{0}\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}

with

\displaystyle f(\eta):=\frac{\eta\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)+\frac{\gamma\eta}{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\alpha_{0}^{2}.

Thus setting the derivatives with respect to
\beta
and
\tau
to zero, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}f^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)-\alpha_{0}\sigma\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\tau}{2}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad f^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)-\alpha_{0}\sigma\sqrt{\gamma}\tau+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad f^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)=\tau^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)}-\frac{1}{2}\right)

and

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}f^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)+\frac{\beta}{2}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad\tau^{2}=2f^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\,.

Combining the latter two we conclude that
\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)=1
. Thus,
\eta=\frac{\beta}{\tau}
is the solution to
\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\eta\right)=1
. To calculate
\tau
and hence
\alpha_{0}
we calculate
f^{\prime}
which is equal to

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(\eta)=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)+\frac{\gamma}{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\alpha_{0}^{2}+\frac{\gamma\eta}{2}\sigma^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma\eta}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,,

where

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\gamma}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\omega_{1}^{2}}{(\omega_{1}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\frac{\omega_{2}^{2}}{(\omega_{2}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\ldots\\
\frac{\omega_{k}^{2}}{(\omega_{k}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta):=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}:=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\,.

Now note that at the optimal point we have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(\eta)=\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}=\frac{\gamma}{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}\,.

Thus from the above we can conclude that

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\eta\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,.

Thus the AO optimization problem reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{b_{\ell}}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\alpha}}\text{ }\frac{\eta\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}{\bm{A}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,,

where
\eta
is the solution to

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\eta}=\gamma\,,

and

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}(\eta):=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}{\bm{I}}_{r}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)\\
\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}:=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\,,

(I.5)

with

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}:=\frac{1}{\gamma}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\omega_{1}^{2}}{\omega_{1}^{2}+\eta}\\
\frac{\omega_{2}^{2}}{\omega_{2}^{2}+\eta}\\
\ldots\\
\frac{\omega_{k}^{2}}{\omega_{k}^{2}+\eta}\end{bmatrix}\,.

First, note that the matrix
{\bm{A}}
is positive definite. This can be checked by computing the Schur complement of
{\bm{A}}
:

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}:=\sigma^{2}{\bm{I}}_{r}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}{\bm{P}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}:=\sigma^{2}{\bm{I}}_{r}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu})-\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\succ\bm{0}_{r\times r}.

(I.6)

Positive definiteness above holds because
{\bm{P}}:=\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu})-\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}\right)\succeq\bm{0}_{k\times k}
. Thus the objective is a strictly convex quadratic and is jointly convex in its arguments. We proceed by minimizing the objective over
(\bm{\alpha},b_{\ell})
which is equal to

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}\\
\widehat{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}=\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}=\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\begin{bmatrix}-\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})\\
1+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})\end{bmatrix}\,.

(I.7)

Thus, the minimum value attained is

\displaystyle-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\begin{bmatrix}-\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}^{T}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}-\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\\
1\end{bmatrix}

(I.8)

\displaystyle=-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(1+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\right).

Thus the objective reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{\eta\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\right)-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\right).

(I.9)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\eta\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\right)-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,,

where

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}\\
\widehat{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}=\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\begin{bmatrix}-\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})\\
1+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})\end{bmatrix}

(I.10)

and

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\gamma}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\omega_{1}^{2}}{(\omega_{1}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\frac{\omega_{2}^{2}}{(\omega_{2}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\ldots\\
\frac{\omega_{k}^{2}}{(\omega_{k}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta):=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}\,.

To continue note that

\displaystyle\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}^{T}&b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\alpha}\\
b_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad=\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}\,.

Thus,

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\eta\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\right)-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{\eta\sigma^{2}\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{(\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\right)}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\right)-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma}{\sigma^{2}\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{(\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\right)}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\right)-\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}\right)

where
\zeta:=\frac{\gamma}{\eta\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{(\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\right)}
.

Asymptotic predictions.
First, from (
I.7
) the bias term converges as follows:

\widehat{b}_{\ell}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(1+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{b}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{P}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\right)\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)\,.

Recall that
\bm{\alpha}={\bm{U}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}
. Thus, the correlations
\langle\bm{\mu}_{i},\bm{w}_{\ell}\rangle,~i\in[k]
converge as follows:

\displaystyle{\bm{M}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}={\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{U}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}=-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right).\quad

(I.11)

Here, convergence applies element-wise to the entries of the involved random vectors. Moreover, from the analysis above we can predict the limit of the norm
\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}
. For this, note that
\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}=\widehat{\alpha}_{0}^{2}+\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}^{T}\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell})+\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{r}\right)\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{2}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\pi_{\ell}^{2}\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}\right)\,.

(I.12)

I.2
Computing
\Sigma_{w,w}

In the previous section we used the CGMT to predict the bias
\widehat{b}_{\ell}
, the correlations
\langle\bm{\mu}_{i},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}\rangle,~i[k]
and the norm
\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}
for all
\ell\in[k]
members of the multi-output classifier. Here, we show how to compute the limits of the cross-correlations
\langle\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\rangle,\ell\neq j\in[k]
.

Lemma I.1

For
\ell\neq j\in[k]
let
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}

\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}
be solutions to the least-squares minimization (
), i.e.,

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j},\widehat{b}_{\ell},\widehat{b}_{j})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad=\arg\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell},\bm{w}_{j},b_{\ell},b_{j}}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\bm{D}\left({\bm{Y}}_{\ell}-{\bm{X}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}-b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\bm{D}\left({\bm{Y}}_{j}-{\bm{X}}^{T}\bm{w}_{j}-b_{j}\bm{1}_{n}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\right\}.

Denote
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell,j}:=\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}
and
\widehat{b}_{\ell,j}:=\widehat{b}_{\ell}+\widehat{b}_{j}
. Then,
(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell,j},\widehat{b}_{\ell,j})
is a minimizer in the following least-squares problem:

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell,j},\widehat{b}_{\ell,j})=\arg\min_{\bm{w},b}\frac{1}{2n}\left\|\bm{D}\left({\bm{Y}}_{\ell}+{\bm{Y}}_{j}-{\bm{X}}^{T}\bm{w}-b\bm{1}_{n}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

(I.13)

Proof

Clearly the minimization in (
I.13
) is convex. Thus, it suffices to prove that
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}
satisfies the KKT conditions. First, by optimality of
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}
, we have that

{\bm{X}}\bm{D}^{2}\left({\bm{Y}}_{\ell}-{\bm{X}}^{T}\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}-\widehat{b}_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}\right)=0

Similarly, for
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}
:

{\bm{X}}\bm{D}^{2}\left({\bm{Y}}_{j}-{\bm{X}}^{T}\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}-\widehat{b}_{j}\bm{1}_{n}\right)=0.

Adding the equations on the above displays we find that

{\bm{X}}\bm{D}^{2}\left({\bm{Y}}_{\ell}+{\bm{Y}}_{j}-{\bm{X}}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell})-(\widehat{b}_{j}+\widehat{b}_{\ell})\bm{1}_{n}\right)=0.

Recognize that this coincides with the optimality condition for (
I.13
). Thus, the proof is complete.

Thanks to Lemma
I.1
, we can use the CGMT to characterize the limiting behavior of
\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}
. Observe that this immediately gives the limit of
\langle\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\rangle
since

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\rangle=\frac{\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}-\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}.

(I.14)

The analysis of (
I.13
) is very similar to that of (
G.1
). In particular we use the following decomposition

\bm{w}_{\ell,j}=\sum_{i=1}^{r}\beta_{i}{\bm{u}}_{i}+\beta_{0}\bm{w}_{\ell,j}^{\perp},

with
\left\|\bm{w}_{\ell,j}^{\perp}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}=1
and
{\bm{U}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell,j}^{\perp}=\bm{0}_{r}
. This allows us to arrive at

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\beta}}\text{ }\min_{\bm{b}_{\ell,j}}\text{ }\max_{\alpha_{0}\geq 0}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\min_{\tau\geq 0}\text{ }\text{ }

\displaystyle\quad\frac{\gamma\beta}{2\tau}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\gamma\beta}{2\tau}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\right)\beta_{0}^{2}-\beta_{0}\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}\,,

where
{\bm{A}}(\eta)
and
\bm{c}_{\ell}
are as in (
I.1
) and
\bm{\nu}(\eta)
is as in (
I.4
).

Setting the derivative of the above with respect to
\alpha_{0}
to zero we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma\beta}{\tau}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\right)\beta_{0}-\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad\beta_{0}=\frac{\tau}{\sigma\sqrt{\gamma}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\right)}\,.

Note that the above objective has the form

\displaystyle g\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)-\beta_{0}\sigma\beta\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\beta\tau}{2}\,,

with

\displaystyle g(\eta):=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma\eta}{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\beta_{0}^{2}.

Thus, the derivatives with respect to
\beta
and
\tau
to zero we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}g^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)-\beta_{0}\sigma\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{\tau}{2}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad g^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)-\beta_{0}\sigma\sqrt{\gamma}\tau+\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad g^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)=\tau^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)}-\frac{1}{2}\right)

and

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}g^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)+\frac{\beta}{2}=0\quad\Rightarrow\quad\tau^{2}=2g^{\prime}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)\,.

Combining the latter two we conclude that
\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}\right)=1
. Thus,
\eta=\frac{\beta}{\tau}
is the solution to
\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}\left(\eta\right)=1
. To calculate
\tau
and hence
\beta_{0}
we calculate
g^{\prime}
which is equal to

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(\eta)=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma}{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\beta_{0}^{2}+\frac{\gamma\eta}{2}\sigma^{2}\beta_{0}^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma\eta}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,.

Here, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\gamma}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\omega_{1}^{2}}{(\omega_{1}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\frac{\omega_{2}^{2}}{(\omega_{2}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\ldots\\
\frac{\omega_{k}^{2}}{(\omega_{k}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta):=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}:=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\,.

Now note that at the optimal point we have

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(\eta)=\frac{\tau^{2}}{2}=\frac{\gamma}{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\beta_{0}^{2}\,.

Thus from the above we can conclude that

\displaystyle\beta_{0}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\eta\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,.

Thus, the AO problem reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{b}_{\ell,j}}\text{ }\min_{\bm{\beta}}\text{ }\frac{\eta\gamma}{2}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)\,,

where
\eta
is the solution to

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\eta}=\gamma\,.

Thus, similar to (
I.10
) we can compute the minimizer of the deterministic

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}\\
\widehat{b}_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}-\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j})\right)\\
\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell})+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j})\right)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\bm{c}_{j}\right)

(I.15)

and

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\gamma}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{\omega_{1}^{2}}{(\omega_{1}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\frac{\omega_{2}^{2}}{(\omega_{2}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\\
\ldots\\
\frac{\omega_{k}^{2}}{(\omega_{k}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta):=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}\,.

To continue note that

\displaystyle\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)\,.

Thus,

\displaystyle\beta_{0}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\eta\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta))}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}^{T}&b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\bm{A}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\beta}\\
b_{\ell,j}\end{bmatrix}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}\Bigg(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}-\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j}\right)\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j}\right)\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\Bigg(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)\Bigg)\,,

where
\zeta:=\frac{\gamma}{\eta\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{k}\frac{\pi_{\ell}\omega_{\ell}^{2}}{(\omega_{\ell}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}\right)}
.
From the CGMT, we have that

\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{0}^{2}+\left\|\bm{\beta}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}.

Combining this with the calculations above, we conclude that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\right)\left(1-\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}-\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j}\right)\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}-\frac{\eta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{r}\right)\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)+\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\Bigg(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}(\eta)+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)+\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta)\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-2\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}^{\prime}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\left(\pi_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}+\pi_{j}\bm{\nu}_{j}\left(\eta\right)\bm{c}_{j}\right)\Bigg)\,.

(I.16)

Finally, using (
I.2
) and (
I.1
) in (
I.14
) it follows that

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{\ell},\bm{w}_{j}\rangle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}

\displaystyle\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{r}\right)\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\bm{c}_{j}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\pi_{\ell}\pi_{j}(\nu_{\ell}\nu_{j}^{\prime}+\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{j})\bm{c}_{j}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}

\displaystyle=\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}+\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\left(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{r}\right)\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}-\bm{e}_{j}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\pi_{\ell}\nu_{\ell}\pi_{j}\nu_{j}\left(\bm{e}_{j}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}\bm{A}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\bm{e}_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}\pi_{\ell}\pi_{j}(\nu_{\ell}\nu_{j}^{\prime}+\nu_{\ell}^{\prime}\nu_{j})\left(\bm{e}_{j}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\bm{e}_{\ell}\,.\right)

(I.17)

Putting everything together we arrive at

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{w},\bm{w}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{P}}+{\bm{P}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\Big(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{r}\Big)\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}{\bm{P}}+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\bm{Q}\,,

where

\displaystyle\bm{Q}:=

\displaystyle\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}\right)+\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\left(\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{A}^{\prime}\bm{A}^{-1}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle-\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)-\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\bm{A}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}\right)

and as mentioned earlier

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}:=

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}(\eta):=\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}^{\prime}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{A}:=

\displaystyle\bm{A}(\eta):=\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\right)\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right)\bm{\pi}\\
\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\nu}(\eta)\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}(\eta)\end{bmatrix}\,.

Let us end by simplifying
\bm{Q}
to this aim

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{I}}&\bm{0}\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}^{T}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{I}}&-\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\\
\bm{0}^{T}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{I}}&\bm{0}\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}^{T}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\bm{I}-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\bm{1}^{T}\right)\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{I}}&\bm{0}\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\bm{I}-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\bm{1}^{T}\right)\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\bm{I}-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\bm{1}^{T}\right)\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\bm{I}-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\bm{1}^{T}\right)+\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}\right)+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\end{bmatrix}

Thus, defining
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}=\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}
we have

\displaystyle\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{diag}\left(\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\\
\bm{1}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}\right)+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left(\text{diag}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}

Using the above and recalling
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}=\bm{\pi}\odot\bm{\nu}^{\prime}
we arrive at

\displaystyle\bm{Q}=

\displaystyle\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\end{bmatrix}^{T}\bm{A}^{\prime}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\\
-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle-\left(\text{diag}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}

\displaystyle-\left(\text{diag}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\right)\bm{V}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{V}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime})-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}

(I.18)

where

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}\bm{1}\right)\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime}\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{\prime T}\bm{1}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\bm{A}=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\sigma^{2}\bm{I}+\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\text{diag}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}\,.

Using the above the cross-correlation matrix
\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{w},\bm{w}}
is given by

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{w},\bm{w}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{P}}+{\bm{P}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\Big(\bm{\Delta}^{-1}-\frac{\zeta}{\sigma^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{r}\Big)\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}{\bm{P}}+\frac{\zeta\eta}{\sigma^{2}}\bm{Q}\,.

Appendix J
Weighted LS for MLM (Proof of Theorem
B.2
)

Let
{\bm{D}}:={\bm{D}}^{(n)}:=\text{diag}(D_{1},\ldots,D_{n})
be a diagonal matrix with non-zero diagonal entries. In particular, assume that the diagonal entries of
{\bm{D}}
are distributed
D_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle iid}}{{\sim}}D
where the random variable
D
may depend on the entries of the matrix of response variables
{\bm{Y}}
. Here, we focus on the following setting:

\displaystyle{\bm{D}}=\sum_{j\in[k]}\text{diag}(\omega_{j}{\bm{Y}}_{j}),\quad\omega_{j}\geq 0,~j\in[k].

(J.1)

Specifically, for (
J.1
), we have
D_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle iid}}{{\sim}}D
with
D=\omega_{\ell}Y_{\ell}+\sum_{i\neq\ell\in[k]}\omega_{i}Y_{i}
, where for all
c\in[k]
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\left([Y_{1},Y_{2},\ldots,Y_{k}]^{T}=\bm{e}_{c}\right)=V_{c}=\frac{e^{\bm{e}_{c}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{g}}}}{\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{k}e^{\bm{e}_{\ell^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{g}}}},

(J.2)

{\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{T}={\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}{\bm{V}}^{T}
, and
{\bm{g}}\sim\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},{\bm{I}}_{r}).

With these, we consider the weighted least-squares (WLS) solution for
\ell\in[k]
:

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{b})=\arg\min_{\bm{w},b}\mathcal{L}_{PO}\left(\bm{w},b\right):=\frac{1}{2n}\left\|{\bm{D}}\left({\bm{X}}^{T}\bm{w}+b\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2},

where
{\bm{D}}
is as in (
J.1
).
In fact, it is convenient to rewrite the above as follows:

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{b})=\arg\min_{\bm{w},b,{\bm{u}}}~\max_{\bm{s}}~\frac{1}{n}\left(\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{D}}{\bm{X}}^{T}\bm{w}+b\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{D}}\bm{1}_{n}\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{D}}\bm{Y}_{\ell}-\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{u}}+\frac{\left\|{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right).

(J.3)

Identifying the AO.
The PO in (
J.3
) is very similar to (
H.1
). In particular, following step by step the same decomposition trick as in Section
G.1.1
, it can be shown that the AO corresponding to (
J.3
) becomes (cf. (
H.6
))

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell},\bm{b}_{\ell},{\bm{u}}}\text{ }\max_{\bm{s}}\text{ }\frac{1}{n}\left(\left\|{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}^{T}{\bm{D}}\bm{s}+\left\|{\bm{D}}\bm{s}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{h}^{T}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}+\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{D}}\widetilde{\bm{G}}^{T}{\bm{U}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{D}}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{s}^{T}{\bm{D}}\bm{Y}_{\ell}-{\bm{u}}^{T}\bm{s}+\frac{\left\|\bm{{\bm{u}}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2}\right),

where we use the same notation as in Section
G.1.1
for
{\bm{P}}^{\perp},{\bm{U}},\widetilde{\bm{G}},{\bm{g}}
and
\bm{h}
. Recall also the relation of
{\bm{Y}}_{\ell}
to
\widetilde{\bm{G}}
in (
H.5
).

Scalarization of the AO.
We start the process of simplifying the AO by setting
\beta:=\left\|{\bm{D}}\bm{s}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\big/\sqrt{n}
and optimizing over the direction of
{\bm{D}}\bm{s}
to equivalently write the AO as

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{w}_{\ell},\bm{b}_{\ell},{\bm{u}}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\beta\left\|\left\|{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\bm{g}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}^{T}{\bm{U}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\beta\bm{h}^{T}{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}\right)+\frac{\left\|\bm{{\bm{u}}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2n},

(J.4)

Next, focus on the minimization over
\bm{w}_{\ell}
. Let us denote

\bm{a}:={\bm{U}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}\quad\text{and}\quad\alpha_{0}=\left\|{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}.

Notice that
\bm{a}\perp{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}
and thus the orthogonal decomposition
\bm{w}_{\ell}={\bm{U}}\bm{a}+{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{w}_{\ell}
. With this observation, note that the optimal direction of
{\bm{P}}^{T}\bm{w}_{\ell}
in (
J.4
) aligns with
{\bm{P}}^{T}\bm{h}
for all values of
\beta
. Therefore, (
J.4
) reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{a},\alpha_{0}\geq 0,\bm{b}_{\ell},{\bm{u}}}\text{ }\max_{\beta\geq 0}\text{ }\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\beta\left\|\alpha_{0}\bm{g}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}^{T}\bm{a}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}-{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}-\beta\alpha_{0}\left\|{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\right)+\frac{\left\|\bm{{\bm{u}}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2n},

(J.5)

Continuing let us denote
\bm{t}:=\alpha_{0}\bm{g}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}^{T}\bm{a}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}
for convenience and rewrite
\left\|\bm{t}-{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}
as follows

\frac{\left\|\bm{t}-{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}}{\sqrt{n}}=\min_{\tau>0}~\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{\left\|\bm{t}-{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}}{2\tau n}.

Note that the resulting minimization is convex in
{\bm{u}}
and concave in
\beta
. Also, by considering the bounded AO (such that
\beta
is bounded; see
[
DKT19
, Sec. A]
), we can flip the order of min-max and optimize over
{\bm{u}}
first. In particular,
{\bm{u}}
minimizes the following strictly convex quadratic

\displaystyle\min_{{\bm{u}}}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\beta}{2\tau}\left\|{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{u}}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}-\frac{\beta}{\tau}\bm{t}^{T}{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right)=\frac{1}{2n}{\bm{u}}^{T}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}{\bm{D}}^{-2}+{\bm{I}}_{n}\right){\bm{u}}-\frac{\beta}{\tau n}\bm{t}^{T}{\bm{D}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}\right\}.

In particular,

{\bm{u}}=\frac{\beta}{\tau}\left(\frac{\beta}{\tau}{\bm{D}}^{-2}+{\bm{I}}\right)^{-1}{\bm{D}}^{-1}\bm{t}=\left({\bm{D}}^{-1}+\frac{\tau}{\beta}{\bm{D}}\right)^{-1}\left(\alpha_{0}\bm{g}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}^{T}\bm{a}+b_{\ell}\bm{1}_{n}-\bm{Y}_{\ell}\right)

Putting things together, the new objective function of (
J.5
) becomes

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{a},\alpha_{0}\geq 0,b_{\ell},\tau>0}~~\max_{\beta\geq 0}~~\mathcal{R}(\bm{a},\alpha_{0},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta)

(J.6)

\displaystyle\qquad\text{where}~~\mathcal{R}(\bm{a},\alpha_{0},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta):=\frac{\beta\tau}{2n}+\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\left\|\bm{t}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}-\frac{\beta}{2\tau n}\bm{t}^{T}\left({\bm{I}}+\frac{\tau}{\beta}{\bm{D}}^{2}\right)^{-1}\bm{t}-\frac{{\beta\alpha_{0}}}{\sqrt{n}}\left\|{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}.

Convergence of the AO

After having simplified the AO into an optimization problem over
r+4
variables, we are ready to study its asymptotic behavior. First, we argue on point-wise convergence of
\mathcal{R}
in (
J.6
). Fix
\bm{a},\alpha_{0},\bm{b}_{\ell},\tau
and
\beta
. From the WLLN,
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\|{\bm{P}}^{\perp}\bm{h}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\sqrt{\gamma}
and as in (
H.9
)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|\bm{t}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\alpha_{0}{\bm{g}}_{i}+\bm{a}^{T}\tilde{{\bm{g}}}_{i}+b_{\ell}-[{\bm{Y}}_{\ell}]_{i}}\right)^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}\right],

where the expectation is over
{\bm{g}}\sim\mathcal{N}(\bm{0}_{r},{\bm{I}}_{r})
(with some abuse of notation) and

\displaystyle Y_{\ell}\sim{\rm Bern}(V_{\ell})\quad\text{and}\quad V_{\ell}=\frac{e^{\bm{e}_{\ell}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{g}}}}{\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}e^{\bm{e}_{\ell^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{g}}}}.

(J.7)

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\bm{t}^{T}\left({\bm{I}}+\frac{\tau}{\beta}{\bm{D}}^{2}\right)^{-1}\bm{t}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left({\alpha_{0}{\bm{g}}_{i}+\bm{a}^{T}\tilde{{\bm{g}}}_{i}+b_{\ell}-[{\bm{Y}}_{\ell}]_{i}}\right)^{2}}{1+\frac{\tau}{\beta}d_{i}^{2}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{1+\frac{\tau}{\beta}D^{2}}\right]

Therefore, point-wise on
\bm{a},\alpha_{0},\bm{b}_{\ell},\tau
and
\beta
, the objective
\mathcal{R}
of the AO converges to

\displaystyle{\cal{D}}_{\ell}(\alpha_{0},\bm{\alpha},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\beta\tau}{2}+\frac{\beta}{2\tau}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}\right]-\frac{\beta}{2\tau}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{1+\frac{\tau}{\beta}D^{2}}\right]-\beta\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{\beta\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{D^{2}\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{1+\frac{\tau}{\beta}D^{2}}\right]-\beta\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{\beta\tau}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{D^{-2}+\left({\tau}/{\beta}\right)}\right]-\beta\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}.

(J.8)

We note that the function above is jointly convex in
(\alpha_{0},\bm{\alpha},b_{\ell},\tau)
and concave in
\beta
.

J.1
Computing
\Sigma_{w,\mu}

It can be checked that the first order optimality conditions of
{\cal{D}}_{\ell}(\alpha_{0},\bm{\alpha},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta)
with respect to
\beta
and
\tau>0
are given as follows:

\displaystyle\beta^{2}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{\left(D^{-2}+\left({\tau}/{\beta}\right)\right)^{2}}\right]\quad\text{or}\quad\beta=0,

(J.9)

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2\beta^{2}}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left({\alpha_{0}G_{0}+\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{\left(D^{-2}+\left({\tau}/{\beta}\right)\right)^{2}}\right].

(J.10)

Thus, at optimality either
\beta=0
or
\tau=\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}
.
In what follows, consider the solution
\tau=\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}
. We will show that this leads to the true saddle point of
{\cal{D}}
.

Moreover, by denoting
\eta:=\frac{\beta}{\tau}
and recalling from (
J.7
) that
Y_{\ell}={\rm Bern}(V_{\ell})
, we can express
{\cal{D}}_{\ell}(\alpha_{0},\bm{\alpha},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta)
as follows

\displaystyle\frac{\beta\tau}{2}+\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]-\beta\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\cdot{\bm{A}}\left(\eta\right)\cdot\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}\left(\eta\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{Y_{\ell}^{2}}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right],

where

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}\left(\eta\right)

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{{\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]&\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{{\bm{g}}}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]\\
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{{\bm{g}}^{T}}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]&\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]\end{bmatrix}

(J.11a)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{{\bm{g}}Y_{\ell}}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]\end{bmatrix}

(J.11b)

we have the following first-order optimality conditions for
\alpha_{0},\bm{a}
and
\bm{b}_{\ell}
:

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle={\bm{A}}^{-1}\left(\eta\right)\cdot\bm{c}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)

(J.12)

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}

\displaystyle=\beta\sqrt{\gamma}\Big/\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right].

(J.13)

Rearranging (
J.13
) and using
\tau=\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}
gives the following equation for
\eta
:

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}}{\beta}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]=\gamma\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau=\alpha_{0}\sqrt{\gamma}}}{{\implies}}~\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1/\eta}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right]=\gamma.

(J.14)

Thus, the optimal values of
\bm{a}
and
\bm{b}_{\ell}
are found by (
J.12
) for
\eta
the positive solution of the equation in (
J.14
). To solve for
\alpha_{0}
, we combine (
J.13
) and (
J.9
) which leads to

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}^{2}\left({\gamma}{\eta^{2}}-\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right)^{2}\right]\right)=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left({\bm{a}^{T}{\bm{g}}+\bm{b}_{\ell}-Y_{\ell}}\right)^{2}}{\left(D^{-2}+1/\eta\right)^{2}}\right],

(J.15)

where we have also used the RHS of (
J.14
). Next, we specialize these findings to the special structure of the weighting matrix
{\bm{D}}
in (
J.1
).

Applying weighting (
J.1
).
Assume (
J.1
) holds.
In this case, Equation (
J.14
) that determines the value of
\eta>0
becomes

\displaystyle F(\eta):=\sum_{i\in[k]}{\frac{\bm{\pi}_{i}\omega_{i}^{2}}{\omega_{i}^{2}+\eta}}={\gamma},

(J.16)

where we have recalled the notation in (
4.1
)
\bm{\pi}_{i}:=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{i}]>0,~i\in[k]
. It can be easily checked by direct differentiation that
\eta\mapsto F
is strictly decreasing in
(0,\infty)
. Also, using
\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\pi}_{i}=1
the range of
F
in
(0,\infty)
is
(0,1)
. Thus, it follows that (
J.16
) has a unique solution for all
\gamma\in(0,1)
.

Also, in this case we can write (
J.11
) in the following more convenient form:

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}\left(\eta\right)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{i\in[k]}\left(\frac{\omega_{i}^{2}\eta}{{\omega_{i}^{2}}+\eta}\right)\underbrace{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{g}}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{g}}^{T}&1\end{bmatrix}V_{i}\right]}_{=:\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{i}}

(J.17)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}\left(\eta\right)

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\omega_{\ell}^{2}\eta}{{\omega_{\ell}^{2}}+\eta}\right)\underbrace{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{g}}\\
1\end{bmatrix}V_{\ell}\right]}_{=:\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{\ell}}.

(J.18)

For convenience let us define vectors
\bm{\nu}:=\bm{\nu}(\eta),\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}=\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}(\eta)\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
with entries:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{i}:=\bm{\pi}_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\gamma}\cdot{\frac{\omega_{i}^{2}}{\omega_{i}^{2}+\eta}}\right)=:\bm{\pi}_{i}\cdot\bm{\nu}_{i}

(J.19)

Because of (
J.16
), notice that
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}
is a probability vector, i.e.

\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{1}_{k}=\bm{\pi}^{T}\bm{\nu}=1.

With the notation above, it holds

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}(\eta)

\displaystyle=\gamma\cdot\eta\cdot\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\nu}_{i}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{i}{\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}]&\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\nu}_{i}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{i}{\bm{g}}]\\
\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\nu}_{i}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{i}{\bm{g}}^{T}]&1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\gamma\cdot\eta\cdot\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\nu}_{i}\cdot\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{i}{\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}]&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\bm{\nu}\\
\bm{\nu}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}

(J.20)

\displaystyle=\gamma\cdot\eta\cdot\begin{bmatrix}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bm{\nu}^{T}\bm{v}\right){\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}\right]&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\bm{\nu}\\
\bm{\nu}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}

(J.21)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}(\eta)

\displaystyle=\gamma\cdot\eta\cdot\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{\ell}{\bm{g}}]\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\gamma\cdot\eta\cdot\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\,\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}

(J.22)

where we have also used the fact that
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[V_{i}{\bm{g}}]=\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\bm{e}_{i},~i\in[k]
and recalled the notation

\bm{v}=[V_{1},\ldots,V_{k}]^{T}.

Using (
J.30
) and (
J.31
), we conclude from (
J.12
) the following expressions for
\bm{a}
and
\bm{b}
:

\displaystyle\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\cdot\left(\bm{e}_{\ell}-\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}\right),

(J.23)

\displaystyle\bm{b}_{\ell}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}-\bm{\nu}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\cdot\left(\bm{e}_{\ell}-\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}\right),

(J.24)

where we defined

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bm{\nu}^{T}\bm{v}\right){\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}\right]-\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\bm{\nu}\bm{\nu}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\succ\bm{0}_{r\times r}.

(J.25)

Finally, we show how to compute
\alpha_{0}
using (
J.15
). The RHS in (
J.15
) can be computed as

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq\ell\in[k]}\frac{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{i}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}}{\left(\omega_{i}^{-2}+1/\eta\right)^{2}}+\frac{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{\ell}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{\ell}+\bm{\pi}_{\ell}}{\left(\omega_{\ell}^{-2}+1/\eta\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\eta^{2}\cdot\gamma^{2}\cdot\left\{{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\left(\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\nu}_{i}^{2}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{i}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}}-2\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{2}\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{\ell}+\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{2}\right\},

where
\bm{a},\bm{b}_{\ell}
are as in (
J.23
) and (
J.24
). Also, note that

\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{D^{-2}+1/\eta}\right)^{2}\right]=\eta^{2}\sum_{i\in[k]}\frac{\bm{\pi}_{i}\omega_{i}^{4}}{\left(\omega_{i}^{2}+\eta\right)^{2}}=\eta^{2}\cdot\gamma^{2}\cdot\bm{\pi}^{T}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\bm{\nu}=\eta^{2}\cdot\gamma^{2}\cdot\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}.

Put together, we have the following expression for
\alpha_{0}
:

\displaystyle\alpha_{0}^{2}
