\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\cdot\left\{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\left(\sum_{i\in[k]}\bm{\nu}_{i}^{2}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{i}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{2}\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{\ell}+\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\cdot\left\{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}{\bm{A}}^{\prime}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\bm{\nu}_{\ell}^{2}\,\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\right\},

(J.26)

where
\bm{a},\bm{b}_{\ell}
are as in (
J.23
), (
J.24
) and we have also defined

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}^{\prime}=\begin{bmatrix}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bm{\nu}^{T}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\bm{v}\right){\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}\right]&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\bm{\nu}\\
\bm{\nu}^{T}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\bm{\nu}^{T}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\bm{\pi}\end{bmatrix}\,.

(J.27)

Asymptotic Predictions.
Writing (
J.24
) in vector form we find that

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{b}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}-\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\left({\bm{I}}_{k}-\bm{\pi}\bm{\nu}^{T}\right)\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\bm{\nu}.

(J.28)

Also, recalling that
\bm{e}_{\ell}^{T}\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{w},\bm{\mu}}=\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}^{T}{\bm{U}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\bm{a}^{T}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}
and using (
J.23
):

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{w},\bm{\mu}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\left({\bm{I}}_{k}-\bm{\pi}\bm{\nu}^{T}\right)\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}.

(J.29)

Finally, for the magnitudes of the weight vectors, recall that
\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\left\|\bm{a}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}+\alpha_{0}^{2}
. Thus, to find the limiting values of the norms, we can combine (
J.1
) and (
J.23
)-(
J.24
). For convenience, we summarize the final expression here. Define the following

Note the slight abuse of notation compared to the definitions in (
J.30
) and (
J.30
). This “renaming” should not be confusing as the constant
\gamma\cdot\eta
(that is different between the two definitions) cancels when computing
\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}={\bm{A}}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}
(see (
J.12
)).

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bm{\nu}^{T}\bm{v}\right){\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}\right]&\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\bm{\nu}\\
\bm{\nu}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&1\end{bmatrix}

(J.30)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{\ell}

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\,\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\,\bm{e}_{\ell}\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}.

(J.31)

Further recall the matrix
{\bm{A}}^{\prime}
in (

).

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\left\|\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\cdot\left(\bm{e}_{\ell}-\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\cdot\left\{\bm{c}^{T}_{\ell}{\bm{A}}^{-1}{\bm{A}}^{\prime}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}^{T}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\right\}.

(J.32)

Remark J.2

Consider the special case
\omega_{i}=1,~i\in[k]
. We show how the above recovers the solution for (un-weighted) LS. First, note that in this case (
J.16
) simply gives

\eta=\frac{1}{\gamma}-1.

Thus,
\bm{\nu}=\bm{1}_{k}
and
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}=\bm{\pi}
. Also, recall that
\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\bm{1}_{k}=\bm{0}
and
\bm{1}^{T}\bm{v}=1
. Thus, (
J.25
) simply gives

\bm{\Delta}=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[{\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}]={\bm{I}}_{r}
. With these, it can be readily checked that (
J.28
) and (
J.23
) simplify to the expressions in (
4.4a
). Similarly,
{\bm{A}}={\bm{A}}^{\prime}={\bm{I}}_{r+1}
and (
J.1
) reduces in this case to (
H.16c
). For general weight coefficients, such simplifications do not seem possible and one needs to compute the matrix
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\bm{\nu}^{T}\bm{v}\right){\bm{g}}{\bm{g}}^{T}\right]
that appears in the definitions of
\bm{\Delta},{\bm{A}}
and
{\bm{A}}^{\prime}
. We note that this calculation can be somewhat simplified by applying Gaussian integration by parts similar to lemma
C.3
.

J.3
Computing
\Sigma_{w,w}

In this section, we use Lemma
G.1
to compute the cross-correlations
\langle\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{j}\rangle,~j\neq\ell\in[k]
. Specifically, the analysis of (
I.13
) is almost identical to the analysis of (
J.3
) in the previous section. Specifically, without repeating all the details for brevity, it can be shown that the AO of (
I.13
) converges to
\min_{\bm{a},\alpha_{0}\geq 0,b_{\ell},\tau>0}~~\max_{\beta\geq 0}~~{\cal{D}}(\bm{a},\alpha_{0},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta)
where
{\cal{D}}(\bm{a},\alpha_{0},b_{\ell},\tau,\beta)
is as in (
J.8
) only with
Y_{\ell}
substituted by
Y_{\ell,c}
:

\displaystyle Y_{\ell,c}\sim{\rm Bern}(V_{c}+V_{\ell})\quad\text{and as before:}\quad V_{i}=\frac{e^{\bm{e}_{i}^{T}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{g}}}}{\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{r}e^{\bm{e}_{\ell^{\prime}}{\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{g}}}},~i=\ell,c.

(J.33)

Thus, what changes in the calculations above is in (
J.18
) and (
J.35
), where we now have instead

\displaystyle\bm{c}\left(\eta\right)

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\omega_{\ell}^{2}}+1/\eta}\right)\underbrace{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{g}}\\
1\end{bmatrix}V_{\ell}\right]}_{=:\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{\ell}}+\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\omega_{c}^{2}}+1/\eta}\right)\underbrace{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{g}}\\
1\end{bmatrix}V_{c}\right]}_{=:\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{c}}

(J.34)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq\{\ell,c\}\in[k]}\frac{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{i}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}}{\left(\omega_{i}^{-2}+1/\eta\right)^{2}}+\frac{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{\ell}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{\ell}+\bm{\pi}_{\ell}}{\left(\omega_{\ell}^{-2}+\eta\right)^{2}}

(J.35)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{A}}_{c}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}\\
\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}-2\begin{bmatrix}\bm{a}^{T}&\bm{b}_{\ell}\end{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{c}}_{c}+\bm{\pi}_{c}}{\left(\omega_{c}^{-2}+1/\eta\right)^{2}},

respectively. With these and following mutatis-mutandis the steps and the notation in the previous section, we find the following asymptotic expression for the magnitude of
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{c}
:

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{c}\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\left\|\bm{\Delta}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\cdot\left(\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\cdot\left(\bm{e}_{\ell}-\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}\right)+\bm{\nu}_{c}\cdot\left(\bm{e}_{c}-\bm{\pi}_{c}\bm{\nu}\right)\right)\right\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\cdot\left\{(\bm{c}_{\ell}+\bm{c}_{c})^{T}{\bm{A}}^{-1}{\bm{A}}^{\prime}{\bm{A}}^{-1}(\bm{c}_{\ell}+\bm{c}_{c})-2\left(\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}+\bm{\nu}_{c}\bm{c}_{c}\right)^{T}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\left(\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{\ell}+\bm{\nu}_{c}\bm{c}_{c}\right)+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{\ell}\cdot\bm{\nu}_{\ell}+\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}_{c}\cdot\bm{\nu}_{c}\right\}.

We may now combine this with (
J.32
) to conclude with the following asymptotic limits for the cross-correlations for all
\ell\neq c\in[k]
:

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{\bm{w}}_{\ell},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{c}\rangle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\bm{\nu}_{c}\left(\bm{e}_{c}-\bm{\pi}_{c}\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\bm{e}_{\ell}-\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\cdot\left\{\bm{c}^{T}_{c}{\bm{A}}^{-1}{\bm{A}}^{\prime}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}-2\bm{\nu}_{c}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\bm{c}_{c}^{T}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\bm{c}_{\ell}\right\}\,.

(J.36)

\displaystyle=\bm{\nu}_{c}\left(\bm{e}_{c}-\bm{\pi}_{c}\bm{\nu}\right)^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\bm{\nu}_{\ell}\left(\bm{e}_{\ell}-\bm{\pi}_{\ell}\bm{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\cdot\left\{\bm{c}^{T}_{c}\left({\bm{A}}^{-1}{\bm{A}}^{\prime}{\bm{A}}^{-1}-2\bm{\nu}_{c}\bm{\nu}_{\ell}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\right)\bm{c}_{\ell}\right\}\,.

(J.37)

In matrix form, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bm{w},\bm{w}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\left({\bm{I}}_{k}-\bm{\pi}\bm{\nu}^{T}\right)\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\Delta}^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\left({\bm{I}}_{k}-\bm{\nu}\bm{\pi}^{T}\right)\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\Big\{\begin{bmatrix}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\end{bmatrix}{\bm{A}}^{-1}{\bm{A}}^{\prime}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad-2\,\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\Big\{\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\begin{bmatrix}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right){\bm{V}}\bm{\Sigma}&\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}\end{bmatrix}{\bm{A}}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Sigma}{\bm{V}}^{T}\left(\text{diag}(\bm{\pi})-\bm{\Pi}\right)\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\\
\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\end{bmatrix}\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\left(1/\gamma-\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}^{T}\bm{\nu}\right)}\Big\{\text{diag}(\bm{\nu})\,\text{diag}(\widetilde{\bm{\pi}})\Big\}\,.

(J.38)
